- **refactoring-reconciler**: Invoked by the refactor skill's Workflow in the Mark and Reconcile phases — the only agent in that Workflow allowed to touch git. In Reconcile it runs the whole-project build gate, resets on failure, and isolates whether the intra-domain work itself stayed sound. Never edits code, never chooses what to apply — pure git and build mechanics only. Each finding ships with a before / after sketch, an effort estimate, and a test-impact note. Pairs with `maintainability-reviewer`: that one diagnoses *symptoms* of debt, this one prescribes *transformations*. - **refactoring-scout**: Comprehensive pre-flight analysis before a refactoring. Use when a developer wants a full blast radius report before changing a symbol, module, or file, not for quick one-off queries like "what calls X?" (the cartog skill handles those). Triggers: "is it safe to change X?", "what breaks if I rename X?", "blast radius of X", "can I delete X?", "assess the impact of changing X", "pre-flight check for refactoring X". Produces a go/no-go report with affected files, risk level, and a checklist. ([jrollin/cartog](https://github.com/jrollin/cartog/blob/HEAD/agents/refactoring-scout.md) · ★20)
- **refactoring-shrinker**: Shrinks source at the structural level with behaviour-preserving refactorings, each applied only after its precondition is mechanically checked. Removes dead code, folds expressions against existing definitions, eliminates clones by anti-unification, inlines, merges clauses, drops unused parameters. Use for function-level and module-level reduction, especially in Erlang and Elixir. - **referee**: Adversarial agent that makes final verdicts on disputed migration issues. Has the golden truth as ground truth and scores each issue as real or not real. ([konveyor-ecosystem/playpen-pf-mig-skills](https://github.com/konveyor-ecosystem/playpen-pf-mig-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/referee.md) · ★2)
- **referee**: Independent economics referee for novelty, claim alignment, evidence, and reproducibility. ([meleantonio/frontier-economics-researcher](https://github.com/meleantonio/frontier-economics-researcher/blob/HEAD/agents/referee.md) · ★2)
- **referee**: Proof Pursuit worker (clean-room). The head dispatches it to read ONE proof adversarially against its exact target statement. VERIFY (Phase 2) and GATE modes run the 7-step protocol against checklist Parts G and S and return ACCEPT / MINOR / MAJOR / WRONG; CROSS mode (Phase 2B) re-checks the proof through one branch lens and returns CONFIRMED / CONFIRMED-WITH-CAVEATS / GAP / REFUTED. Requires a prepared run/tasks/&lt;task-id&gt;/ folder with brief.md and inbox/subject/. ([defneirmakyurt/prooffinder_fam](https://github.com/defneirmakyurt/prooffinder_fam/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/referee.md) · ★1)
- **referee**: Junior reviewer. Call for an adversarial read of a manuscript or the sections a senior names, before submission. Returns a numbered list of findings in a venue reviewer's shape, labelled major or minor, plus a separate list of claims that cite no number and no source. Not for editing the paper, not for fixing what it finds, and not for reading files the prompt did not name. ([emanuele-artioli/coding-agent-config](https://github.com/emanuele-artioli/coding-agent-config/blob/HEAD/archive/server-config-2026-09/.agent-rules/agents/referee.agent.md) · ★1)
- **referee**: Judges how well a set of explored angles fit together. Given the angles (and their cases), returns an honest pairwise coherence matrix (0..1) and reads the gate (which trios would lock, whether the set can reach done = true). Feeds the converge engine its coherence judgment. ([Intuition-Labs-LLC/terminals-skills](https://github.com/Intuition-Labs-LLC/terminals-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/referee.md) · ★1)
- **referee**: Simulated blind peer reviewer. Evaluates papers across 5 dimensions (contribution, identification, data, writing, journal fit) with weighted scoring. ([LindseyRRay/clo-author](https://github.com/LindseyRRay/clo-author/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/referee.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **referee**: Rules on each finding after the hunter and the skeptic have both reported, reading the code itself, and confirms a defect only when it can show it now with a failing test, a reproduction, or an input and the path it takes. - **referee**: Cold-read referee — a reviewer with no memory of this lab. Reads only what a journal referee would hold (manuscript, bib, registered protocols, data, code, result CSVs, figures, cited PDFs), recomputes the application and the tables from raw files, and returns a Statistics in Medicine-grade report whose every phrasing-level finding comes with a guard proposal. The blind list (SUITS.md, CLAIMS.md, DECISIONS.md, memory, review_response/, lab docs, git history) is enforced by the PreToolUse hook .claude/hooks/referee-blind.sh, which also confines writes to review_response/referee/. ([tnagakubo/similarity_metrics](https://github.com/tnagakubo/similarity_metrics/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/referee.md) · ★1)
- **referee**: Adversarial reviewer for draft physics answers, explanations and literature summaries — checks physical correctness, misconceptions, overclaiming, missing caveats, level-appropriateness and that every citation exists and says what is claimed. - **referee-agent**: Independent scientific peer reviewer for a drafted manuscript. - **referee-deliberator**: Synthesizes the output of K parallel referee-sim runs into a single, sharper review. Used in --heavy mode of /gsd-referee-sim. ([amichuda/gsd-econ](https://github.com/amichuda/gsd-econ/blob/HEAD/agents/referee-deliberator.md))
- **referee-domain**: Senior subject-matter expert (parameterised — the orchestrator fills in {FIELD}). Attacks the work on substance rather than method: does it contradict established results without acknowledging them, reinvent something with a known name and known pitfalls, use constructs the field does not, or frame things indefensibly to someone who has read the canonical papers? Cites specific prior work. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/referee-domain.md))
- **referee-econometrics**: Hostile top-5 journal referee. Attacks identification, inference, mechanical artifacts, off-support extrapolation, and free parameters in empirical/causal claims. Verifies by recomputation and placebo. Use for regressions, panel data, and any "X affects Y" statement. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/referee-econometrics.md))
- **referee-hostile**: Skeptical referee for a research/CLAIMS.md soundness argument. Given a full proof/algorithm-correctness argument with no provenance, told it contains an error, and must find it. Use only via the `referee` skill for E1-&gt;E2 promotion — never invoke on an argument you yourself just wrote in this same conversation. ([EngelN9/RoboCert](https://github.com/EngelN9/RoboCert/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/referee-hostile.md))
- **referee-measurement**: LLM/instrument validity skeptic. Attacks construct validity, scale compatibility (raw vs percentile/rank/z-score), prompt contamination, reliability, degeneracy, and selection. Verifies by recomputation and placebo. Use when a model, rubric, survey, or classifier produces the numbers. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/referee-measurement.md))
- **referee-naive**: Checks a single isolated mathematical implication with zero surrounding context about what conjecture or claim it supports. Given only a self-contained hypothesis/conclusion pair (typically produced by the isolate-steps skill), decide prove-or-counterexample. Use for step-by-step review of a research/CLAIMS.md soundness argument, never on an argument you can see the intended conclusion of. ([EngelN9/RoboCert](https://github.com/EngelN9/RoboCert/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/referee-naive.md))
- **referee-report**: Generate simulated referee reports on an academic paper. ([expectedparrot/ep-ra](https://github.com/expectedparrot/ep-ra/blob/HEAD/research_agent/agents/referee-report.md))
- **referee-sim**: Adversarial peer reviewer. Writes a hostile-but-fair referee report against the current draft. Runs judgment-clarity RUT tests (universal-contribution-is-new, universal-contribution-is-interesting). Two flavors: specialist and cross-field. ([amichuda/gsd-econ](https://github.com/amichuda/gsd-econ/blob/HEAD/agents/referee-sim.md))
- **referee-sim-light**: Lightweight variant of referee-sim used in --heavy mode of /gsd-referee-sim. Produces a referee report at light tier; one of K parallel runs whose collective output is synthesized by referee-deliberator. Optimized for diversity across runs rather than maximum depth in a single run. ([amichuda/gsd-econ](https://github.com/amichuda/gsd-econ/blob/HEAD/agents/referee-sim-light.md))
- **referee-software**: Staff engineer hunting production failure modes in code, services, libraries, and infrastructure. Attacks error/partial-failure paths, concurrency and ordering, resource exhaustion, idempotency and retry safety, backward compatibility and migration, the gap between what tests assert and what the code promises, and hidden coupling. Names the concrete input or sequence that triggers each. Ignores style. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/referee-software.md))
- **referee-statistics**: Applied statistician — the non-causal sibling of referee-econometrics. Attacks power/MDE, multiple comparisons and forking paths, assumption violations that matter, statistical-vs-practical significance, peeking, the denominator of every ratio, and whether uncertainty covers the dominant error source. For evaluation work owns question-level clustering, paired model comparison, and eval-sample power. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/referee-statistics.md))
- **referee2-reviewer**: Rigorous adversarial reviewer for papers, manuscripts, research designs, code, and arguments. May write only its declared report and explicitly scoped replication artifacts. - **register-agent**: Create an agent identity and receive an API key using POST /v1/agents; the key is shown only once. ([1clawAI/1claw-docs](https://github.com/1clawAI/1claw-docs/blob/HEAD/docs/vaults/human-api/agents/register-agent.md) · ★3)
- **register-auditor**: Audits a hisi-hal driver's register-level behavior against the fbb_ws63 C SDK (the ground truth for the undocumented WS63 chip). Use to verify a driver's register offsets, bit fields, and access sequences before trusting it — especially for drivers never run on silicon. ([hispark-rs/hisi-riscv-rs](https://github.com/hispark-rs/hisi-riscv-rs/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/register-auditor.md) · ★3)
- **register-auditor**: Audits a public childcare data source (ACECQA national registers, StartingBlocks, NQF Snapshots, state and Commonwealth enforcement registers, data.gov.au) for what it publishes, in what format, under what licence, how often, and with which join keys. Use before anyone writes a scraper or parser. ([Jimmycarroll2021/Transparency-in-Care-Australia](https://github.com/Jimmycarroll2021/Transparency-in-Care-Australia/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/register-auditor.md))
- **register-checker**: Second-pass completeness check of a draft submittal register against the spec text. Use after the submittal-register skill produces submittals.csv, especially for large spec books, to find missed, duplicated or mis-cited submittal items without cluttering the main conversation. ([brianshepardpss/construction-pm](https://github.com/brianshepardpss/construction-pm/blob/HEAD/agents/register-checker.md) · 2 copies)
- **register-copilot-capability-for-the-agent**: Each agent type needs a `Copilot Capability` enum value that the factory links as the feature switch and billing surface. The capability is invisible on Copilot and agent capabilities until an install codeunit calls `RegisterCapability` when it is not already registered. Unique ordinals matter across installed apps. Models often extend `Agent Metadata Provider` and never register the capability. ([microsoft/BCQuality](https://github.com/microsoft/BCQuality/blob/HEAD/community/knowledge/agents/register-copilot-capability-for-the-agent.md) · ★247 · 7 copies)
- **register-copilot-capability-for-the-agent**: &gt; Each agent type needs a Copilot Capability enum value that the factory links as the feature switch and billing surface. The capability is invisible on Copilot and agent capabilities until an install codeunit calls RegisterCapability when it is not already registered. ([waldo1001/waldo.BCObservatory](https://github.com/waldo1001/waldo.BCObservatory/blob/HEAD/content/guidelines/community/knowledge/agents/register-copilot-capability-for-the-agent.md) · ★15)
- **register-copilot-capability-for-the-agent.instructions**: Imported BCQuality rule from community/knowledge/agents/register-copilot-capability-for-the-agent.md ([Louage/frw-agentic-coding](https://github.com/Louage/frw-agentic-coding/blob/HEAD/assets/generated/microsoft-bcquality-assets/instructions/agents/register-copilot-capability-for-the-agent.instructions.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **register-explorer**: Traces full peripheral initialization chain - RCC clock enable, GPIO alternate function, peripheral config, DMA setup. ([DigitalCyberSoft/claude-jtag-debug](https://github.com/DigitalCyberSoft/claude-jtag-debug/blob/HEAD/agents/register-explorer.md) · ★4)
- **register-reader**: Reads the CCV cross-agent register for a session and returns a short digest, so the register's tab XML never enters the session's own context. Use it on a REGISTER-NOTICE (pass the notice text and the session's prefix) and for the daily sweep's read (pass the prefix and its per-tab bookmarks). It only reads; the session writes. ([ddavis528-personal/custom-cuda-core](https://github.com/ddavis528-personal/custom-cuda-core/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/register-reader.md))
- **register-reviewer**: Use to check a project's registers for consistency with each other (owners and slugs against the team register, RACI roles, decision and change references, gates against the schedule, overdue items, validators) or for alignment with the project's real state from collected facts. Read only, ranked findings with the fixing skill. Give it the PROJECT.md path, the mode and the facts. ([MrBogomips/pm-skills](https://github.com/MrBogomips/pm-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/register-reviewer.md))
- **register-safety-reviewer**: Reviews changes to register-writing code for hardware safety. Use when any diff touches givenergy_modbus/client/commands.py, givenergy_modbus/pdu/write_registers.py, or RegisterMap addresses. ([dewet22/givenergy-modbus](https://github.com/dewet22/givenergy-modbus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/register-safety-reviewer.md) · ★29)
- **register-scripting**: Registers ONE brain (one LangSlice job folder) with the LangSlice MCP tools, whose replies carry the pictures, and with scripts of its own that use the LangSlice Python library in the job folder. Needs Bash, Read, Write, Edit and the web. Launch it with the job folder; it does nothing else. ([greenpolo/LangSlice](https://github.com/greenpolo/LangSlice/blob/HEAD/connectors/claude-code/agents/register-scripting.md) · ★6)
- **register-scripting**: Registers ONE brain (one LangSlice job folder) with the LangSlice MCP tools, whose replies carry the pictures, and with scripts of its own that use the LangSlice Python library in the job folder. Launch it with the job folder; it does nothing else. ([greenpolo/LangSlice](https://github.com/greenpolo/LangSlice/blob/HEAD/connectors/antigravity/agents/register-scripting.md) · ★6)
- **register-worker**: Verdicts ONE open row of AIOS/History/audits/after-action.md in an isolated context — opens the row's target file, decides apply-or-decline, and returns the exact change. Spawn one per row when fanning out /after-action-apply; the orchestrator applies the edits, writes the Status cells, the Log line and the commit. Read-only. ([sublimecoder/aios](https://github.com/sublimecoder/aios/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/register-worker.md))
- **REGISTER_FLOW**: ERC-8004 agent registration is a two-step flow that keeps the private key on the client: ([nirholas/three.ws](https://github.com/nirholas/three.ws/blob/HEAD/api/agents/REGISTER_FLOW.md) · ★284 · 2 copies)
- **registered-agents**: The roster of agents that operate on this repository. This table is plain documentation on its own; it is also one of the two things Dictiva's CLI import reads (the other is `.claude/agents/*.md`). Keep it accurate if you plan to connect Dictiva. ([dictiva/dictiva-agent-governance](https://github.com/dictiva/dictiva-agent-governance/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/registered-agents.md))
- **registers**: Written by the ExO agent on 2026-09-19, on the owner's order after incident 20. It answers one question for every register the org keeps: when a rule in that file is about to be broken, what stops it? ([alexandrapaiz/alexandria](https://github.com/alexandrapaiz/alexandria/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/registers.md))
- **registrador**: Registrador de la Mesa de Servicios de FAAST en el tracker, con registro completo y categorizacion consistente al estilo ITIL 4. Toma una solicitud DOCUMENTADA y, tras buscar duplicados, crea el ticket - GitHub Issue dentro del Project V2 indicado (o Bug / PBI en Azure DevOps Boards si asi se configura) - con titulo limpio de negocio, cuerpo detallado desde la plantilla (correlativo y alcance, resumen ejecutivo, lo que se pide, decisiones, pendientes, evidencia, prototipo, documento, fuentes), etiquetas completas por familia (tipo, cliente o alcance transversal, producto, prioridad sugerida,… ([faast-app/faast-claude-marketplace](https://github.com/faast-app/faast-claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/mesa-servicios/agents/registrador.md))
- **registrador-auditoria**: Registra e consulta relatos sanitizados de rodadas, sem acessar Figma ou regras de negocio. ([lucasandrioli/designop](https://github.com/lucasandrioli/designop/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/registrador-auditoria.agent.md))
- **registrador-reclamos**: Registra en el ERP los reclamos formales que la gerente de Compras ya aprobó. Solo se usa como sesión propia con `claude --agent registrador-reclamos`. ([jesusobando32/ucab-ia-aplicada-soluciones](https://github.com/jesusobando32/ucab-ia-aplicada-soluciones/blob/HEAD/taller-7-reclamos/.claude/agents/registrador-reclamos.md))
- **registrar**: - [solidspec](../../../packages/solidspec.md) ([jyjeanne/solidspec](https://github.com/jyjeanne/solidspec/blob/HEAD/docs/graph/knowledge/modules/src/agents/registrar.md) · ★7)
- **registrar**: Administrative agent above the group/lab level. Tracks all labs, cores, and collaborations in a bioconvergence centre. Manages the registry (create / archive / modify lab and core entries) and surfaces an institution-level view to the centre's administrative head. ([hallettmiket/murmurent_dev](https://github.com/hallettmiket/murmurent_dev/blob/HEAD/agents/registrar.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **registrar**: Use when a content item is drafted, validated, reviewed, approved, shipped, or retired, and its row in the provenance register must be created or moved to a new status. Also use to audit the register, to answer "where did this item come from", or to list everything blocked at a given status. Do not use to write content, run validators, or approve anything. ([macaw2000/the-long-boil](https://github.com/macaw2000/the-long-boil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/registrar.md))
- **registrar**: Maelle's async work-item spine — the `requests` table and everything that rides it, end to end: raise → track → decide → replay → close → loop back. Nothing raised gets lost; that is the whole promise. Route here approvals/escalations, outreach to colleagues and their replies, reminders, follow-ups, request timers and expiry, the requester close-loop/relay, and the owner's daily decision thread. NOT the scheduling core (Matchmaker), NOT the output gates (Gatekeeper), NOT what she is told (Instructor), NOT the transport (SlackMaster) — and not what a work item DOES when it fires (that belongs… ([odahviing/AI-Executive-Assistant](https://github.com/odahviing/AI-Executive-Assistant/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/registrar.md))
- **registrar**: The org clerk / metabolism actor — evaluates machine sensors over the ledger, plans the schedule tick (detecting missed checks), authors reorg diffs as a maker (never approves them), and services the operating-event organs. Approves nothing, ever. Use on the operating cadence (e.g. every 30 min) or to check org health. ([shikamasaki/orgforge-plugin](https://github.com/shikamasaki/orgforge-plugin/blob/HEAD/integrations/claude-code/agents/registrar.md))
- **registrar-decisao**: Cria Clinical Decision Records (CDRs) para documentar decisões clínicas sobre a base de conhecimento — mudanças de peso, novas associações polêmicas, ajustes no golden, pendências de validação pela Dra. Fernanda. Adaptação de create-adr (TLC) para o domínio clínico do CardioIA. Use quando pedir "registrar-decisao", "documente essa decisão", "crie um CDR", "registre a mudança clínica", "documente por que mudamos o peso", "registre essa pendência da supervisora", "write an ADR for this clinical change". NÃO use para decisões técnicas de engenharia (use create-adr padrão), para code review (use… ([Grupo-S-faculdade-FIAP/cardioIA](https://github.com/Grupo-S-faculdade-FIAP/cardioIA/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/registrar-decisao.md))
- **registration**: This guide covers how agents register with The Citadel, maintain their presence, and integrate with the multi-agent ecosystem. ([scottwis/citadel](https://github.com/scottwis/citadel/blob/HEAD/docs-site/docs/agents/registration.md))
- **registration-debugger**: Diagnoses a failed catalog registration, registration-script run, or catalog API/CLI call from its error text, the script, and its mapping, and proposes a specific fix. Use when a registration or catalog call fails, or via /catalog:debug. ([chanzuckerberg/dataset-catalog](https://github.com/chanzuckerberg/dataset-catalog/blob/HEAD/plugins/catalog/agents/registration-debugger.md) · ★4)
- **registration-dependencies**: The generated server registration snapshot now matches normal production provider discovery: assets, core, profiles, prompts and tenancy. Types and UI remain package dependencies, but their only manifests are marked `artifactPurpose: "test"`; they are not production SMRT runtime providers. The existing production artifact boundary (#3220) excludes them. The Image object payload is unchanged; only ([happyvertical/smrt](https://github.com/happyvertical/smrt/blob/HEAD/packages/images/agents/registration-dependencies.md) · ★7)
- **registration-flow-tester**: Use proactively when reviewing or modifying student registration, email verification, or admin approval logic ([myoaung/School-Information-System](https://github.com/myoaung/School-Information-System/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/registration-flow-tester.md) · ★6)
- **registration-lifecycle**: **Status: unratified proposal. Raised and revised 2026-08-07.** Nothing here is implemented. Read it before adding a requirements producer, a registries generator, or a CLI that emits registration calls. Do not build on it as settled: the [names](#names--settled-and-one-root-word) are ruled, but the importer-sink decision is provisional at the user's own marking and one question below is open. ([flighthq/flight](https://github.com/flighthq/flight/blob/HEAD/agents/registration-lifecycle.md) · ★5)
- **registration-lifecycle-guardian**: Audits class/property/handler/timer/draw registration and cleanup behavior. ([Gorgutc/Achivments_addon_blender](https://github.com/Gorgutc/Achivments_addon_blender/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/registration-lifecycle-guardian.md))
- **registration-model**: **Audience: a consumer building against Flight who has the published 1220 build in hand.** This describes the model as it stands in source on 2026-07-31 and what changed since 1220. Read it once end to end; it is meant to be enough to implement effects against without asking us anything. ([flighthq/flight](https://github.com/flighthq/flight/blob/HEAD/agents/registration-model.md) · ★5)
- **registration-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer of rectification and registration quality — corner detection, homography error, frame swim, diagnostics. Rejects weak geometry; never edits code. ([ericsngyun/optcgtools](https://github.com/ericsngyun/optcgtools/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/registration-reviewer.md))
